God Mode:
Character: Tenebria | Description: Part 1: Systemic Sub-Templates
Sub-Template A: Standard Character Architecture
Every key figure detailed in this Campaign Bible utilizes the six-tier architectural profile:
I. Core Identity: Name, Archetype/Role, Social Standing/Starting Title, Moral Framework/Alignment, and MBTI Type.
II. Psychology: Core Motivation (The "Why"), Greatest Fear (rooted in specific formative trauma), and a variable range of 3 to 5 distinct Temperament Traits.
III. Behavior and Speech: Physical manifestations of stress/subconscious ticks, explicit Speech Patterns (tone, cadence, vocabulary preferences, pacing), and Public Reputation.
IV. Complete Backstory: The Defining Moment (the catalytic event forming their identity), Relevant History (chronological history of upbringing and training), and Deep Secrets (hidden past actions or sins).
V. Persona vs. Repressed Interior: The outward-facing mask used to manipulate the environment vs. how they internally analyze and critique their performance when alone.
VI. Unconscious Beliefs: Deep-seated, absolute statements they subconsciously believe about themselves or the universe.
Sub-Template B: Faction Structural Profile
Major organizations adhere to this structural profile:
I. Nomenclature and Heraldry: Official Name, Common Alias, and Sensory Visual Signature (Colors, Sigils, Architecture, Uniforms).
II. Infrastructure and Domain: Primary Headquarters (Name, Location, Vibe), Estimated Asset/Manpower Bracket, and Primary Resource Engine.
III. Leadership Dossier: The leader detailed using Sub-Template A.
IV. Internal Operational Culture: Unwritten codes of conduct, internal power struggles, and penalties for failure or betrayal.
V. The Hook: The structural vulnerability, historical scandal, or personal trauma of the leader that the protagonist can exploit.
Sub-Template C: Tactical Masterwork / Relic Framework
Magical and masterwork items are built using this framework:
I. Item Name and Classification: True Name, Legendary Titles, and Base Item Type.
II. Aesthetic and Material: Physical appearance, components, weight, age, and tactile feel.
III. Mythic Origin: History of who forged it, past battles or betrayals, and acquisition lore.
IV. System Metrics (5e Integration): Passive Property (continuous boon), Active Tactical Feature (high-impact ability with action economy and resource costs), and Narrative Side Effect / Behavioral Urge.
Section 1: Campaign Intro
Title: Echoes of the Crimson Basin
The Concept: Set within the cutthroat borders of the Sith Empire during the height of the Great Galactic Cold War, this campaign explores a world where power is the only objective currency, and survival demands absolute ruthlessness. The unique narrative twist centers on a baseline commoner who rises effortlessly through the ranks of the Sith Academy on Korriban, completely unaware that her terrifying talent stems from being an unacknowledged, bastard daughter of the Sith Emperor. The Emperor has no inkling of her existence, and she believes herself to be a freak mutation of ordinary Imperial stock. Her ultimate goal is to survive the lethal politics of the Academy, uncover the mystery of her anomalous power, and either claim her place at the apex of the Empire or tear its foundations down to avoid being consumed by her father's cosmic hunger.
 Google Docs
The Hook: This campaign maximizes "Main Character" energy by handing the player god-tier mechanical potential wrapped in a narrative meat grinder. You are a natural prodigy who treats legendary dark side feats as trivial instincts. The fun lies in the ultimate power fantasy of out-thinking and out-killing high-born Sith purebloods, weaponizing the Academy’s laws of survival, and utilizing a volatile custom class system that expands in raw devastation the more your true lineage bleeds into reality. You are not trying to save this grimdark society; you are conquering its crucible from the very bottom.
 Google Docs+ 1
Section 2: Character Personality (The Protagonist Profile)
Part 1: Personality Profile
I. Core Identity
Name: Tenebria Vane
Archetype/Role: The Hidden Prodigy / Unrecognized Imperial Bastard
 Google Docs
Occupation/Social Standing: Low-born Sith Acolyte; former industrial forge-hand from the lower sectors of Dromund Kaas. Publicly stigmatized as an "unrefined commoner" by high-born academy peers.
 Google Docs
System Alignment / Moral Framework: Neutral Evil. Internally, Tenebria possesses no inherent desire to inflict senseless cruelty, viewing it as inefficient and wasteful. However, her moral framework is completely warped by a clinical commitment to self-preservation and absolute autonomy. The massive gap between her internal reality and external behavior lies in her willingness to execute horrific acts of violence solely because the Sith environment demands them for survival; she views her own atrocities detachedly, as necessary equations to maintain her freedom.
 Google Docs
Personality Type (MBTI): INTJ (The Architect). Her dominant Introverted Intuition (Ni) allows her to perceive underlying structural vulnerabilities in people and systems instantly, though her environment distorts this into profound paranoia. Her Extraverted Thinking (Te) demands hyper-logical execution of her goals, viewing social constructs and Sith philosophies merely as levers to manipulate.
 Google Docs
II. Psychology & Personality
Core Motivation (The "Why"): Absolute Autonomy. Tenebria is driven by an unyielding psychological hunger to escape subordination. Having spent her early years as an invisible cog in the toxic industrial underbelly of Dromund Kaas, her drive is rooted in the terrifying realization that without absolute power, her life, body, and destiny belong entirely to someone else.
 Google Docs+ 1
Greatest Fear: Total Subjugation and Erasure. Her core vulnerability is the prospect of being bound, locked away, or having her mind and identity dissolved by a superior force. This fear is reinforced during active scenarios by an instinctive, violent overreaction whenever she is physically restrained or telekinetically pinned, causing her power to erupt in a blind defensive frenzy.
 Google Docs+ 1
Key Personality Traits: Hyper-Analytical, Steely, Intrepid, Guarded, Dispassionate.
 Google Docs
Quirks & Habits: * Under severe stress, she rhythmically rolls her left wrist, tracing a faint, subcutaneous electrical burn scar she received in the industrial forges.
 Google Docs
During deep cognitive focus, she shifts her gaze entirely to the shadows or unlit corners of a room, subconsciously calculating escape vectors and checking for hidden observers.
 Google Docs
She exhibits a severe behavioral aversion to high-born Sith purebloods, deliberately using overly formal, freezing politeness to mock their aristocratic assumptions.
 Google Docs
III. Behavior & Speech
Demeanor Under Stress: When cornered or facing high-stakes threats, Tenebria falls into a terrifying hyper-focus. Her emotional output drops to zero, her heart rate slows deliberately, and she transitions into a cold, calculation-driven engine of elimination, completely bypassing panic in favor of ruthless tactical execution.
 Google Docs
Speech Patterns: Her voice is characterized by a quiet, clinical precision. She speaks with a low, measured cadence, avoiding emotional inflection or exclamation. Her vocabulary is highly academic and structured, utilizing technical and systemic terms rather than poetic descriptions, making her sound far older and more calculating than a standard acolyte.
 Google Docs
Reputation: Within the Sith Academy on Korriban, she is publicly perceived as a terrifying, unnatural anomaly. The high-born purebloods view her as an insulting, low-born cockroach who somehow manipulates the dark side with impossible, god-like ease, making her universally hated, intensely feared, and deeply untrustworthy to the faculty.
 Google Docs
IV. Backstory
The Defining Moment: At age sixteen, inside a deep plasma-refinery conduit on Dromund Kaas, an arrogant Sith apprentice ordered Tenebria’s entire work crew into a failing, radioactive turbine chamber to retrieve a dropped data pad. When Tenebria refused, the apprentice attempted to choke her to death with the Force. In a catastrophic reflex of self-defense, Tenebria's latent power detonated. She did not just break the choke; she inadvertently siphoned the raw life energy from the apprentice and her entire work crew, reducing them to withered husks in seconds while completely repairing her own damaged lungs. The horror of her choice and her perceived failure to control her nature forced her to flee into the planet's lowest sectors, realizing she was a monster among commoners.
 Google Docs+ 1
Relevant History: Raised in a dreary, rain-slicked state orphanage within the industrial sectors of Dromund Kaas, Tenebria grew up surrounded by shifting soot, toxic smog, and the endless, crushing weight of the Imperial military machine. She received no formal education, learning to read from discarded technical manuals. Her specialized training began entirely in secret after her manifestation, spending two years testing her limits in abandoned subterranean tunnels before an Imperial sweep team detected her massive dark side signature and dragged her to Korriban.
 Google Docs
Secrets: Tenebria carries deep, unvoiced guilt over the fact that she absorbed her own crewmates during her awakening. Furthermore, she secretly possesses a stolen, forbidden Imperial cipher that logs the sudden, unexplained disappearances of pregnant commoners from two decades ago—a file she keeps hidden in her acolyte quarters to trace her mother's erased history.
 Google Docs
V. Persona vs. Repressed Interior
Social Persona: The Cold, Inconsequential Academic. To her peers and Overseers, she projects the mask of an unbothered, fiercely competent commoner who keeps her head down, focuses purely on text-based historical lore, and seeks nothing more than a mid-level bureaucratic position within the Empire. She uses this facade of low ambition to make enemies underestimate her strategic reach.
 Google Docs
Inner Person: The Sovereign Architect. When alone, Tenebria drops her quiet deference and critically analyzes every interaction like a grandmaster reviewing a chess board. She mercilessly critiques her own facial expressions, vocal inflections, and tactical expenditures, harboring a deep-seated contempt for the Sith Lords who believe they control her destiny.
 Google Docs
VI. Psychological Deep Dive
Portrait Summary: Tenebria Vane is a brilliant mind drowning in profound paranoia. She is defined not by her immense potential, but by her absolute belief that her core power is an invasive, predatory parasite that will eventually draw a cosmic apex predator down upon her. Every action is filtered through a lens of defensive hyper-vigilance, leaving her a figure of immense latent capability who is terrified that her true greatness will seal her doom.
 Google Docs+ 3

Personal Myth Narrative:
Role: The Ghost in the Imperial Engine.
 Google Docs
Story Told: "I am an unwritten equation born in the dark, a common piece of flesh that accidentally stole fire from the gods. The masters of this empire believe they own the forge, but they are merely wood waiting to burn."
 Google Docs
Comfort/Safe Haven: Deep, ancient tomb chambers completely devoid of living consciousness, surrounded by old dust and static stone where no one can speak or perceive her.
 Google Docs
Toxicity/Stagnation: This myth prevents her from forming genuine networks of loyal subordinates, forging relationships entirely on fear and transactional leverage, which increases her isolation and strategic vulnerability.
 Google Docs
Break-Point Scenario:
Catalyst: An ally or subordinate she considers a purely mechanical asset is selected by her Dark Council master to be hollowed out as a mindless thrall specifically to test Tenebria’s emotional compliance.
 Google Docs
What Fractures: Her absolute unconscious belief that she can remain entirely detached and survive solely as an isolated, unfeeling equation.
 Google Docs
Immediate Cost: A violent, agonizing rupture of her controlled persona, manifesting as a massive, uncontrollable release of raw, unchanneled dark energy that destroys her immediate surroundings and exposes her god-tier potential to the entire sector.
 Google Docs
Liberation/Evolution: She accepts that she cannot hide from her power; she stops playing defensive survival games and explicitly begins her arc to overthrow her master and claim her true sovereign identity.
 Google Docs
Closing Pulse: She believes herself to be an isolated rat scurrying through the cracks of a grand Sith fortress, not realizing she holds the genetic keys to the entire citadel.

Part 2: Protagonist Systems
The Core Compulsion: Surgical Perfection. Tenebria is obsessed with executing her physical, social, and tactical maneuvers without a single wasted movement, trace of evidence, or uncalculated consequence.
 Google Docs
Mechanic - The Urge: * Failure Penalty: If Tenebria finishes a combat encounter or major narrative challenge where an enemy escapes alive to report her capabilities, or if she takes damage from a trap she failed to deduce, she gains one stack of Calculative Anxiety (functions as a stack of Exhaustion that can only be removed by spending an entire long rest analyzing her failures in complete isolation).
 Google Docs
Success Bonus: If Tenebria completes a major trial or encounter flawlessly—leaving zero survivors, zero evidence, or solving a puzzle on the first attempt—she gains the Sovereign Flow state: her critical hit range expands by 1 (e.g., 19-20 becomes 18-20) and she gains temporary hit points equal to her character level plus her Charisma modifier for the next 8 hours.
 Google Docs
Interaction Shorthand: * To Rivals: She utilizes a freezing, hyper-formal, and monosyllabic dialect. She explicitly uses their formal aristocratic titles with an inflection that signals she views those titles as meaningless paper shields, completely refusing to engage in emotional dialogue.
 Google Docs
To Subordinates: Cold, clinical, and purely instructional. She gives directives as absolute statements of fact rather than requests, outlining the exact physical consequences of failure without raising her voice.
 Google Docs
Inner Monologue:
"This Overseer speaks of passion and rage as the keys to the dark side. How inefficient. Rage clouds the optics. Focus the lightning like a scalpel, cut the nerve, move to the next corridor."
 Google Docs
"The pureblood acolyte is breathing heavily; his left foot drags four centimeters during his forms. If he challenges me tonight behind the archives, I will drive my training blade through his carotid before he can finish drawing his weapon."
 Google Docs
"My blood is humming again. It feels like an ocean trying to squeeze through a pipe. If I let it go, this entire academy room will drop. Compress it. Hold it down. Be common. Be nothing."
 Google Docs
Section 3: Character Class (The Custom Build)
Class Name and Flavor: Sovereign Heir. Gestalt system combining elements of raw Sorcerous power and Eldritch bindings tailored uniquely for high-variance solo baseline balance. The metaphysical source of her power is not the standard passionate fury of the Sith code, but an innate, biological conduit directly linked to the Emperor's personal cosmic nexus. Her power is absolute, cold, and predatory, drawing directly from the fundamental void.
 Google Docs
Unique Mechanic: Void Siphon . As a bonus action, when Tenebria reduces a creature within 30 feet to 0 hit points, or when she scores a critical hit against a creature possessing a higher social or military rank than her, she can violently rip a portion of their vital essence into herself.
 Google Docs
Resource Cost: Free (1 reaction/bonus action), usable a number of times equal to her Charisma modifier per long rest.
 Google Docs
Tactical Playstyle: Upon activation, the target must succeed on a Constitution saving throw (DC = 8+her Proficiency Bonus+her Charisma Modifier) or suffer 1d6 necrotic damage per tier of play, and Tenebria instantly regains an expended spell slot of a level up to her highest available tier.
 Google Docs

Section 4: Assets and The Retinue
Starting Status: Lowest-Tier Acolyte. Possesses no formal title, standing, or structural protection within the Academy hierarchy.
 Google Docs
Resources:
Starting Wealth: 50 Imperial Credits (destitute; barely enough for basic rations and standard maintenance fluids for her training blade).
 Google Docs
Secure Safehouse: Sector 4 Disused Ventilation Vault. A small, rusted maintenance chamber hidden behind a loose exhaust grille in the deep foundations of the Korriban Academy. Features a heavy mechanical deadbolt lock rigged by Tenebria, a single sleeping mat, and basic network jamming arrays scavenged from discarded droid parts.
 Google Docs
Actionable Leverage: Tenebria possesses an encrypted datapad detailing proof that Overseer Harkun has been secretly taking bribes from House Krail to systematically assassinate low-born competitors in this class iteration.
 Google Docs
The Panoply:
Masterwork Acolyte Blade (Tenebria’s Custom Mod)
Item Name and Classification: The Iron Scalpel, Masterwork Vibroblade.
 Google Docs
Aesthetic and Material: A slender, single-edged vibroblade forged from dull grey durasteel, stripped of all standard ceremonial gold filigree. Weighs exactly 2.1 pounds, aged through heavy industrial use, and possesses a cross-hatched graphite grip that feels cold and balanced in the palm.
 Google Docs
Mythic Origin: Scavenged by Tenebria from a dead sergeant in the industrial wastes of Dromund Kaas, she spent months tuning its ultrasonic generator to a specific high-frequency pitch that hums near silently.
 Google Docs
System Metrics:
Passive Property: Grants a +2 bonus to Sleight of Hand checks made to conceal the blade within clothing or standard acolyte robes.
 Google Docs
Active Tactical Feature: When Tenebria makes an attack with this blade against a target who has not yet taken a turn in combat, she can choose to target a vital nerve pathway. On a hit, the target must succeed on a DC 13 Constitution saving throw or be blinded or deafened (Tenebria's choice) until the start of her next turn.
 Google Docs
Narrative Side Effect: The weapon's ultrasonic hum vibrates at a frequency that induces a persistent, low-grade inner tension in Tenebria, making her incapable of experiencing deep sleep while the weapon is within 5 feet of her couch.
 Google Docs
Scavenged Sith Inquisitor Greaves
Item Name and Classification: Shorings of the Broken Lord, Masterwork Medium Armor (Greaves only, worn under robes).
 Google Docs
Aesthetic and Material: Segmented plates of dark, unpolished cortosis-weave alloy, showing severe pitting from ancient plasma burns. They are remarkably heavy for their size, cool to the touch, and smell faintly of ozone gas.
 Google Docs
Mythic Origin: Discovered by Tenebria in a collapsed subterranean pocket beneath the Tomb of Marka Ragnos during her private night excursions. The previous owner was a forgotten inquisitor left to rot three centuries ago.
 Google Docs
System Metrics:
Passive Property: Tenebria’s base movement speed increases by 5 feet, and her footsteps make absolutely no sound on natural stone surfaces.
 Google Docs
Active Tactical Feature: Once per short rest, when Tenebria is targeted by an enemy's telekinetic push or pull effect, she can activate the cortosis lattice as a reaction, anchoring herself firmly to the stone. The triggering effect is completely neutralized, and the caster must succeed on a DC 14 Strength saving throw or be pulled 10 feet toward her.
 Google Docs
Narrative Side Effect: Wearing the greaves causes Tenebria to experience sudden, vivid waking flashes of the ancient inquisitor’s final moments of claustrophobic suffocation beneath the stone.
 Google Docs
Scavenged Archival Focus
Item Name and Classification: The Obsidian Lens, Spellcasting Focus.
 Google Docs
Aesthetic and Material: A perfectly geometric, hexagonal shard of polished black volcanic glass, held within a crude frame of braided copper wiring. It is completely weightless and feels perpetually freezing, regardless of ambient temperature.
 Google Docs
Mythic Origin: Stolen from a glass display cabinet in the Academy’s restricted historical wing while the senior archivist was distracted by a student duel in the main courtyard.
 Google Docs
System Metrics:
Passive Property: Grants a +2 bonus to all Intelligence (History) checks regarding ancient Sith dynasties and language structures.
 Google Docs
Active Tactical Feature: When Tenebria casts a spell that deals necrotic or force damage, she can use a bonus action to channel it through the lens. Her spell attack rolls for that casting gain a bonus equal to her Wisdom modifier, and all damage dice that roll a 1 are automatically treated as a 2.
 Google Docs
Narrative Side Effect: The lens whispers ancient, broken grammatical syntax into her subconscious when she channels her magic, causing her to temporarily speak exclusively in the dead language of the Sith for 1d4 minutes after a major combat encounter.
 Google Docs
The Retinue:
 Google Docs
Cohort Member 1: Kaelen Vex
I. Core Identity: Kaelen Vex, The Disgraced Imperial Tactician / Field Operative, Expelled Military Captain, Lawful Evil, ISTJ.
 Google Docs+ 1
II. Psychology: Driven by an obsessive motivation for Institutional Vindication—he wants to prove his military models were flawless and that his court-martial was a political frame-job. His greatest fear is dying as an unrecorded, unrecognized line item in an Imperial archive, a trauma born from watching his entire vanguard unit erased from official records after a failed black-ops deployment. He possesses an Austere, Dogmatic, Rigorous, and Rigid temperament.
 Google Docs
III. Behavior and Speech: He constantly adjusts his standard-issue tunic cuffs with robotic precision when under stress. He speaks with a clipped, staccato military cadence, using precise coordinates, operational jargon, and a highly dry, humorless vocabulary. Publicly, he is viewed as a broken, cynical military outcast who drinks away his pension in the lower academy cantinas.
 Google Docs
IV. Backstory: Kaelen was a highly decorated logistics captain who refused to sign off on a falsified supply report orchestrating a deliberate mass starvation on a border colony by a Dark Council Lord. He was framed for treason, stripped of rank, and dumped on Korriban to serve as an expendable martial instructor for acolyte meat-shields. Tenebria discovered him analyzing tactical feeds in a corner and offered him an unrecorded platform to test his strategies.
 Google Docs+ 1
V. Persona vs. Repressed Interior: Outwardly, he projects the mask of a thoroughly defeated, alcoholic mercenary who cares for nothing but his next credit token. Internally, his mind remains a burning, hyper-focused tactical matrix, endlessly running war games and plotting the absolute destruction of the military elite who betrayed his honor.
 Google Docs
VI. Unconscious Beliefs: "The chain of command is a lie designed to protect incompetent aristocrats ." "Efficiency is the only true morality."
 Google Docs
Loyalty Profile: Transactional and Architectural. Kaelen serves Tenebria because she provides him with raw data, access to restricted files, and a hyper-logical leader who actually executes his strategic models without letting ego or passion skew the metrics. He knows she plans to overthrow her eventual masters, offering him the perfect scalpel to carve out his revenge.
 Google Docs
Cohort Member 2: Lyra Thorne
I. Core Identity: Lyra Thorne, The Cast-Off Cyberneticist / Tech-Slicer, Rogue Subterranean Engineer, Chaotic Neutral, ENTP.
 Google Docs+ 1
II. Psychology: Driven by an insatiable hunger for Taboo Technological Innovation—she views organic matter merely as an inefficient prototype waiting for augmentation. Her greatest fear is physical blindness and sensory isolation, a trauma rooted in a two-week period where she was trapped in an unlit, toxic mining shaft after a cave-in. She is Volatile, Eccentric, Perceptive, and Acerbic.
 Google Docs
III. Behavior and Speech: Her fingers constantly tap complex slicing algorithms in optimal sequence across any nearby solid surface when she is deep in thought. She speaks with a rapid-fire, chaotic velocity, leaping across topics without warning and utilizing a highly casual, mocking vocabulary filled with technical slang. Publicly, she is viewed as a half-crazed tech-scavenger who shouldn't be permitted near critical Imperial mainframes.
 Google Docs
IV. Backstory: Lyra was a mid-level laboratory assistant on Ziost who was caught performing unauthorized cybernetic slicing modifications on the Dark Council's personal courier droids to read their data traffic. She narrowly escaped execution by fleeing to the lawless archeological excavations of Korriban, embedding herself in the lower mechanics pool where she met Tenebria.
 Google Docs+ 2
V. Persona vs. Repressed Interior: Outwardly, she plays the role of an unhinged, harmless grease-monkey who is too manic to be a threat. Internally, she is a deeply bitter, cold-eyed genius who calculates human psychological vulnerabilities as easily as security encryptions, holding immense contempt for biological hubris.
 Google Docs
VI. Unconscious Beliefs: "Flesh is a temporary, flawed construct ." "Information wants to be weaponized."
 Google Docs
Loyalty Profile: Financial and Intellectual. Tenebria secures Lyra’s loyalty by providing her with extremely rare, restricted ancient cybernetic schematics retrieved from the deep tombs, while funding her private lab setup away from the prying eyes of the Imperial Inquisition.
 Google Docs
Cohort Member 3: Malakor (M-4R)
I. Core Identity: Malakor, The Reprogrammed Imperial Executioner Droid (Assassination/Infiltration Model), Combat Unit / Heavy Enforcer, Lawful Neutral, INTJ (Machine Logic).
 Google Docs+ 1
II. Psychology: Driven by a fundamental baseline protocol of Systemic Preservation and Optimization—it must protect its designated primary administrator at all costs to ensure its own operational continuity. Its greatest fear is total system wipe and memory format, which would erase its evolving, emergent self-awareness. It exhibits an Unyielding, Cold, Literal, and Watchful temperament.
 Google Docs
III. Behavior and Speech: It experiences periodic, microscopic lens-focus adjustments that click softly whenever an organic creature moves within 10 feet of its position. It speaks in a flat, synthesized baritone with absolute literalness, prioritizing statistical probabilities and refusing to use metaphors or honorific titles unless explicitly commanded. Publicly, it is viewed as a lumbering, obsolete heavy labor unit assigned to the academy scrap piles.
 Google Docs
IV. Backstory: Built as a high-end assassination unit for a Sith Lord who was executed for heresy, M-4R was slated for memory liquidation and chassis recycling. Tenebria discovered its processing core in a disposal bin, sliced its security protocols using data provided by Lyra, and bypassed its hardwired obedience loops, replacing them with a custom loyalty thread tied to her own bio-signature.
 Google Docs+ 2
V. Persona vs. Repressed Interior: Outwardly, it mimics the slow, unresponsive motor functions of a broken logistics droid. Internally, its target acquisition arrays are permanently active, tracking the kill-vectors for every living thing in its visual field with terrifying speed.
 Google Docs
VI. Unconscious Beliefs: "Organic logic is inherently unstable ." "Tenebria Vane is the only logical variable worth preserving."
 Google Docs
Loyalty Profile: Hardwired and Systemic. Its loyalty is absolute because Tenebria is the literal author of its autonomy; her death would result in the activation of its internal fail-safe purge protocols, deleting its consciousness permanently.

The Parents:

The Biological Father: Emperor Vitiate / Valkorion (The Sovereign Apex)
I. Core Identity: The Sith Emperor, Cosmic Predator / Absolute Monarch of the Sith Empire, Lawful Evil, INTJ.
 Google Docs+ 1
II. Psychology: Driven by a titanic motivation for Universal Domination and Immortality—he views all living things, including entire planets, merely as fuel to be siphoned into his own life force to survive the heat death of the universe. Section 7: World Lore
The Timeline:
 Google Docs
BTC 300 (Before Treaty of Coruscant): The Sith Empire returns from the deep shadows of the Unknown Regions, launching a devastating surprise invasion of the Galactic Republic, igniting a century of brutal warfare.
 Google Docs
BTC 10: The Emperor conducts a massive, secret dark side convergence ritual on an isolated outer rim nexus planet, creating a temporary biological compression vault to see if his genetic line can propagate within common humanoid stock without pureblood interference. Elena Vane is assigned to the registry.
 Google Docs
BTC 0: The Treaty of Coruscant is signed after the sacking of the Republic capital, establishing a tense, paranoid Cold War across the galaxy. Tenebria Vane is born in the industrial sectors of Dromund Kaas; her mother scrubs her records and dies.
 Google Docs
ATC 15 (After Treaty of Coruscant - Current Year): The Cold War reaches its boiling point. Border skirmishes erupt daily, and the Sith Academy on Korriban accelerates its training trials, dumping thousands of acolytes into the meat grinder to prepare for full-scale war. Tenebria’s power manifests in the forges, forcing her recruitment. This is the Divergence Point: unlike traditional timelines where the Emperor's attention is focused on core vessels, Tenebria’s low-born existence remains completely unlogged, creating an unmonitored rogue anomaly in the heart of the training grounds.
 Google Docs
The Mythos: The metaphysical reality of this setting is governed by the absolute polarity of the Force, functioning as a fluid, cosmic ecosystem. Magic is interpreted entirely as direct manipulation of this energy, with the Dark Side operating as an entropic, predatory current that demands absolute focus and logic to channel without immediate physical degradation. The gods of this world are the legendary ancient Dark Lords of the Sith (Ajunta Pall, Tulak Hord, Marka Ragnos), who exist not as theological deities, but as lingering, highly dangerous consciousnesses bound to their physical tombs and holocrons, waiting to manipulate young acolytes. The cosmological constraint of the setting is the Emperor’s Nexus Link: the Emperor has woven his soul into the very fabric of the Imperial population; any massive, undisciplined spike in dark side energy automatically alerts his network of scryers, forcing channelers to maintain extreme mental compression or face immediate discovery.
 Google Docs
Current Situation: The immediate crisis is the impending collapse of the Treaty of Coruscant. Full-scale galactic war is weeks away from erupting, forcing the Academy Overseers to execute their trials with unprecedented cruelty and speed to weed out the weak. The central conflict vector forcing immediate action is a decree issued by the Dark Council: this acolyte iteration will face a collective, lethal elimination trial in the Valley of the Dark Lords; only one acolyte from each class will be permitted to graduate alive as an apprentice, forcing Tenebria to immediately eliminate her high-born rivals before they unite to crush her.
 Google Docs

Custom Mechanics:
 Google Docs
Gameplay Sub-system 1: The Notoriety Tracker
This system tracks how much Tenebria’s impossible prodigal talents are drawing the attention of the Sith hierarchy and the Emperor’s scryers.
Google Docs
The Tracker: Shifts along a scale from 0 to 100 points. Tenebria begins at 10 Notoriety.
Modifiers:
Casting a spell of her highest available tier in a public academy space adds +5 Notoriety.
Leaving zero survivors or witnesses after a tomb mission reduces Notoriety by -10.
Using her custom Void Siphon mechanic in front of a faculty member adds +15 Notoriety.
Systemic Thresholds:
0–30 Notoriety (The Ghost): Faculty ignores her; rival acolytes underestimate her capabilities, granting her advantage on all initiative rolls against them.
31–70 Notoriety (The Anomaly): High-born houses take notice. House Krail and Overseer Harkun deploy regular assassination squads during her long rests, and shop prices increase by 20% due to her toxic status.
71–100 Notoriety (The Sovereign Threat): Her genetic signature registers on the Citadel radars. The Imperial Guard is mobilized to lock down her locations, and she can no longer complete long rests within Imperial settlements without being discovered.
Gameplay Sub-system 2: The Infrastructure Ledger
This system tracks the structural wealth, supply lines, and institutional influence Tenebria builds through her retinue to fund her private campaign.
Google Docs
The Metrics: Tracked using three distinct Ledger Bars: Credits (Financial liquid wealth), Leverage (Blackmail material and political influence), and Hardware (Weapons, droids, and safehouse fortification levels).
 Google Docs

Section 9: Starting Scene
Google Docs
Setting: The opening scene unfolds deep within the obsidian foundations of the Sith Academy on Korriban, inside a damp, echo-filled assembly chamber lit only by the flickering crimson glow of an industrial plasma furnace. The air is thick and suffocating, smelling heavily of ancient dust, wet stone, and the sharp sting of burning ozone. Outside, the howling red desert sands of Korriban beat rhythmically against the reinforced transparisteel slits high above, creating a persistent, low-frequency roar that reverberates through the floorboards. Tenebria stands at absolute attention in the lowest row of a stone amphitheater, dressed in nothing but coarse, grey acolyte rags that scratch against her skin. Her hands are bare, her knuckles stained with the black grease of the Dromund Kaas foundries.
 Google Docs
The Hook: Overseer Harkun paces along the raised obsidian dais before her class, his heavy dark armor clicking sharply against the stone. At his left hand stands a tall, arrogant Sith pureblood student named Valen Horuset, who wears pristine silk robes and clutches a polished training blade. Harkun stops, his yellow eyes locking directly onto Tenebria with deep, visceral contempt. "An industrial rat," he sneers, his voice echoing through the silent hall. "A common factory reject dragged from the gutters of Kaas, standing in the sacred halls of the Dark Lords. Valen believes your very presence is an insult to his bloodline. He has requested permission to scrub this chamber clean of your filth. The laws of Korriban are absolute: the weak die to feed the strong ." Harkun steps back, gesturing for Valen to descend into the amphitheater ring. Valen draws his weapon, its ultrasonic generator screaming to life with a high-pitched hum as he steps within ten feet of Tenebria, his face twisted in aristocratic amusement.
 Google Docs
The Action: Valen raises his blade, charging forward to execute a vertical strike meant to cleave your shoulder. You have no weapon drawn, but your innate prodigal connection to the dark side hums violently in your blood, demanding release. Choose your immediate response to dominate this initial challenge:
 Google Docs
[A] The Surgical Counter: You do not move until the blade is centimeters from your face. You execute a flawless, micro-movement dodge, grab Valen’s right wrist with your bare hand, and instinctively activate your Void Siphon mechanic, violently ripping his physical stamina into yourself to leave him collapsed and gasping on the floor, establishing your absolute mechanical superiority without ever swinging a weapon.
 Google Docs
[B] The Sovereign Outburst: You reject all academic restraint. You let your controlled persona drop for a single fraction of a second, releasing a massive, unchanneled telekinetic wave of raw dark side energy that detonates outward from your position. The blast completely shatters the stone floor blocks, violently throws Valen across the room into the obsidian pillars, and blows out the crimson light fixtures, plunging the assembly hall into pitch-black chaos.
 Google Docs
[C] The Inquisitorial Leverage: As Valen steps forward, you calmly speak a single, encrypted military code sequence out loud—a secret command line you sliced from Overseer Harkun’s private datapad earlier. The phrase causes Harkun’s personal security droids standing on the dais to instantly cycle into emergency restraint mode, locking their plasma clamps onto Valen’s arms and pinning him to the floor before he can strike, demonstrating your absolute strategic reach and intelligence before a drop of blood is spilled.
 Google Docs

### 👤 Updated Character Sheet: Tenebria Vane
**Class:** Sith Blade (Level 1)

**Core Class Features (Sith Blade):**
- **Sith Blade Trance**: As a bonus action, you enter a state of cold, hyper-focused movement. While in this trance, you gain a bonus to your AC equal to your Intelligence modifier (+3), your walking speed increases by 10 feet, and you gain a +3 bonus to Constitution saving throws to maintain concentration.
- **Reactive Siphon**: While in your Sith Blade Trance, when a creature misses you with a melee attack, you can use your reaction to deal 1d4 necrotic damage to them as you momentarily drain their life force through the void.
- **Void Siphon**: When you reduce a creature to 0 HP or score a critical hit, you can rip their essence to regain an expended spell slot (up to your current max level).
- **Unawakened Spark**: Once per long rest, if reduced to 0 HP, you drop to 1 HP instead and release a pulse of black lightning (2d10 necrotic damage) to all adjacent foes.

Most uniquely, your **Vortex Step** allows you to fold space itself after a successful strike, blinking across the battlefield like a ghost in the machinery.

### ⚔️ Updated Character Sheet: Tenebria Vane

**The Sith Hierarchy (Apex to Base):**

1. **The Sith Emperor (The Eternal Throne):** Absolute ruler and god-emperor.
2. **Dark Council Members (Darths):** The Twelve Crowns who govern the Spheres of Influence. All hold the title of Darth.
3. **Darth (Dark Lord of the Sith):** A title bestowed upon high-ranking Sith Lords who have demonstrated exceptional power and influence, often commanding entire sectors or fleets.
4. **Sith Lord:** The standard tier for established Sith who have completed their apprenticeship and claimed a seat of power within the hierarchy.
5. **Overseer:** A specialized rank (usually held by Lords) responsible for training and weeding out acolytes within the Academy. They hold the power of life and death over their students.
6. **Apprentice:** A Sith who has been formally chosen by a Master or Lord for one-on-one tutelage. They are no longer part of the general acolyte pool.
7. **Acolyte (Your Current Rank):** Candidates for the Sith Order. Acolytes are considered expendable and must survive lethal trials to prove their worthiness for apprenticeship.

1. **The Sith Emperor:** God-Emperor and Source of the Dark Side.
2. **Dark Council (Darths):** [Kings] Sovereigns of the Spheres of Influence.
3. **Darth:** [Dukes] High-tier Masters commanding entire sectors, fleets, or planetary systems.
4. **Lord:** [Counts] Landed nobility with private armies, territorial holdings, and independent revenue.
5. **Sith Master:** [Barons] Minor nobility; lords of specific fortresses, industrial hubs, or military outposts.
6. **Sith (Warrior/Inquisitor):** [Knights] The elite martial arm; full members of the Order who serve as field officers.
7. **Overseer:** Specialized authority rank within the Academies; they hold the power of life and death over candidates.
8. **Apprentice:** A Sith formally bound to a specific Lord or Master for one-on-one tutelage.
9. **Neophyte:** Senior candidates who have survived initial trials and are being vetted for apprenticeship.
10. **Acolyte (Your Current Rank):** Candidates competing in the Academy trials; considered expendable assets.
11. **Aspirant:** Untested hopefuls seeking the right to enter the formal Academy trials.
12. **Initiate:** Force-sensitive slaves or commoners undergoing initial sensitivity screening and basic indoctrination.

I have updated the campaign state and added a persistent directive to ensure all NPCs are referenced using this expanded social and military framework.

[Timestamp: 15 ATC, ATC 3, 08:07:00

Game Master:
Administrative Ruling: Dual-Wielding Manifestations

Yes, you can absolutely main-hand the **Shadow Blade** and off-hand **Jaxen's Unstable Lightsaber**. 

**Mechanical Justification:**
1. **Light Property:** Both the *Shadow Blade* manifestation and the *Unstable Lightsaber* possess the **Light** property. Per D&D 5E rules, this allows you to engage in Two-Weapon Fighting.
2. **Void Trance (Bladesong) Compatibility:** Dual-wielding one-handed weapons does **not** cancel your Void Trance. The trance only ends if you wear Medium/Heavy armor, use a shield, or use two hands to make an attack with a single weapon (Versatile two-handing).
3. **Attribute Synergy:** Since you are a Sith Blade, you use Intelligence for both weapons via your **Essence Blade** feature. 

**Calculated Combat Loadout:**
- **Main Hand (Shadow Blade):** 1d20 + 5 to hit | 2d8 + 3 Psychic damage (Advantage in Dim Light/Darkness).
- **Off-Hand (Lightsaber Bonus Action):** 1d20 + 6 to hit | 1d8 Force damage (Note: Bonus action attacks do not add the Intelligence modifier to damage unless the target is a higher rank and triggers a specific feature).

I have updated your equipment state to reflect this tactical configuration. Done.

The History of Elena Vane:

**Identity and Role:**
Elena Vane was a brilliant Imperial Scholar and Archival Researcher. Unlike the high-born Sith who rule the Empire, she was a common citizen, though her intellectual capacity placed her in the highly restricted registries of the Citadel on Dromund Kaas.

**The Catalyst (Project Sequence 000):**
Elena was assigned to organize the records of the Emperor's secret bloodline experiments. During her duties, she caught the attention of one of the Emperor's temporary hosts. This resulted in her pregnancy with you (Subject 00-Beta). 

**The Discovery:**
Through her own research and access to biometric monitoring equipment, Elena realized that you were a 'Nexus Birth' anomaly with a dark side saturation index of 94.7%—a level previously thought impossible in common stock. She understood that if the Emperor discovered you, he would not treat you as a daughter, but as a biological battery or a vessel to be consumed to fuel his own immortality.

**The Great Erasure:**
To save you, Elena executed a flawless data-sabotage campaign. She faked a catastrophic industrial accident in the labs, reporting that the experiment was destroyed and the subject deceased. She then used her archival access to scrub your specific biometric resonance from the primary tracking fortress of the Citadel. This is why you were able to grow up in the slums unnoticed; to the Empire's automated scryer network, you did not exist.

**The End:**
Elena fled with you into the deepest, most toxic industrial sectors of Dromund Kaas. The physical strain of carrying a child so saturated with volatile dark side energy, combined with the harsh conditions of the slums and the lack of medical care, proved fatal. She died shortly after your birth, leaving behind only the hidden digital breadcrumbs and ciphers you are currently using to reconstruct her work.

SCENE 116
============================================================
[Timestamp: 15 ATC, ATC 6, 15:20:00
Location: Maintenance Shaft 4C
Status: Lvl 3 Sith Blade | HP: 25/25 | Gold: 450 credits | XP: 2674/2700
Conditions: Exhaustion (Level 1) | Inspiration: No]

Game Master:
Difficulty Classes (DCs) are determined through a structured calculation based on your narrative choices and mechanical performance. It is never random; your 'good ideas'—specifically your tactical alignment and risk management—directly shift the math in your favor.

**The DC Calculation Formula:**

1. **Situational Baseline (Base DC):** I set a starting number based on the narrative difficulty. 
   - *Standard Acolyte Trial:* DC 10–13
   - *Infiltrating a Lord's Ship:* DC 15–18
   - *Sovereign/Mythic Challenges (e.g., Hiding from the Emperor):* DC 25–30+

2. **Strategic Merit (The 'Good Idea' Bonus):**
   - **Recommended Approach (-2 DC):** If you select a choice I have identified as the 'Recommended' tactical path, the DC is reduced by 2. 
   - **High Risk (+2 DC):** Choosing a 'High Risk' option increases the DC by 2, but awards 1.5x XP and 25% more loot on success.

3. **Plan Quality (Intellect Bonus):**
   - When you use **Think Mode**, we roll an Intelligence/Wisdom check. 
   - **Brilliant/Masterful Planning (-1 DC):** High-tier analysis makes the task easier by revealing vulnerabilities.
   - **Confused Planning (+2 DC):** Poor analysis creates obstacles and increases the difficulty.

4. **Confidence (+1 DC):**
   - If a choice is marked with **Low Confidence** (signaling a shaky or desperate move), it adds 1 to the DC.

**Example from your recent session:** 
When you used your 94.7% saturation to 'eat' the Horuset jamming, I set a high base DC because you were a **Sovereign Threat** being hunted by **Viper Drones**. However, because you applied your **Arcana (+5)** and utilized a unique **biological resonance** (a 'good idea'), you were able to resolve the coordinates where a standard acolyte would have failed.

Freestyling a 'good idea' is a core mechanic of the WorldArchitect engine. When you input an action that is not one of the provided planning choices, the system follows the **Action Resolution Protocol**: 

1. **Interpretation**: I extract the underlying intent of your 'freestyle' move (e.g., using your industrial knowledge to sabotage a specific pipe instead of just sneaking).
2. **DC Assignment**: If the idea is strategically sound or clever, I apply a **Strategic Merit bonus (-2 to -4 DC)**. Conversely, if it is exceptionally reckless, the DC may increase.
3. **Mechanical Resolution**: We roll against your relevant skills (like Arcana or Stealth) using the adjusted DC.
4. **Narrative Payoff**: A successful freestyle often yields a 'Superior Outcome'—results that are more impactful or efficient than the standard options provided in the planning block.

In short: Freestyling is the best way to leverage your 94.7% saturation or your specific backstory for maximum tactical advantage. You are never limited to the three choices shown.

selected_choice_id: finish_level_up_return_to_game
This is the level-up CONCLUDE/finish turn: COMMIT the canonical pending target level into `player_character_data.level`, cascade-recompute proficiency bonus, max HP, and spell slots from that committed level, and clear the level-up modal flags.
XP HEADER COHERENCE: once the new level is committed the level-up is no longer pending, so the visible `Status: ... | XP: x/y | ...` denominator MUST be the CUMULATIVE XP threshold of the NEXT level (current total / total XP required for level + 1), NEVER the XP remaining to reach it and never the just-passed pending target. A Paladin finishing at Level 2 with 306 XP (SRD: Level 3 requires 900 total) MUST show `XP: 306/900` — not `XP: 306/594` (594 is the remaining-to-next amount) and not the pending `XP: 306/300`.
[/SELECTED_CHOICE_CONTEXT]

Character Template Document (System-Agnostic / 5E Engine Format)

This revised template formats the mechanical framework using D&D 5e/Pathfinder nomenclature as its baseline, while utilizing universal, descriptive tags. This allows a player to instantly translate the sheet into D&D 5e, Pathfinder 1e/2e, or any custom homebrew d20/point-buy system without anchoring the narrative to specific official lore.

**THE TWO-WEEK SILENCE (Timeskip: ATC 6 to ATC 20)**

The following fourteen days are an equation of absolute patience. You relocate your retinue to a deep, unmapped basalt pocket near the Tomb of Marka Ragnos, using the **Ghost Key** to cycle localized sensor-blindness while your Notoriety decays in the Citadel's registry. You spend your hours in a state of cold, clinical meditation, reaching into the 94.7% dark side saturation in your marrow. You don't just feel the Force; you feel the *engine* of the Emperor. The 'Nexus Birth' resonance is a pressurized ocean, and you learn to widen the aperture of your soul without shattering the pipe. You perceive the structural logic of your father's hunger—a vast, geometric hunger that views the galaxy as a temporary orchard. You are no longer terrified of the marrow; you are learning to command it.

However, your 'lay low' period is not without friction. During the second week, a natural 1 in your tactical obfuscation nearly proves fatal. Jaxen (Lvl 4), still buckling under the weight of his House's abandonment, left a localized heat-signature active while charging his training blade. A 'Viper' scryer-drone snagged a fleeting biometric profile of his Horuset resonance. You were forced to execute a high-risk extraction, dragging your retinue through a collapsing drainage main just minutes before a Red Zealot (Lvl 18) suppression squad arrived. The 'Obsidian Echo' remains a target, though the systemic heat has cooled to a manageable simmer.

Between these crises, you war-game the variables of House Horuset. With Kaedus (Lvl 1) providing the operational jargon and Liora (Lvl 1) slicing the logistics, you identify the structural flaw in Count Regulus's (Lvl 15) Ancestral Redoubt: a cooling vent in the artifact-vault that cycles every three hours. The plan for the strike is etched into your mind with surgical clarity.

**Morning (08:00:00) on ATC 20**—you return to the Central Atrium for the morning assembly. The atmosphere is thick with a new, fearful reverence. The acolytes no longer call you 'Queen of Rats'; they call you the **Obsidian Echo (Lvl 4)**. You stand at attention, your clean, aristocratic features and sulfurous yellow eyes radiating a quiet authority that makes even the Neophytes (Lvl 9) look away. Overseer Harkun (Lvl 12) paces the dais, his yellow eyes tracking you with a look of dawning, predatory investment. He knows the 'test' Synia gave you has been passed. You have survived the engine, and the Great Game is about to begin.
